public class AuthorsResourceParameters
{
    private const int MaxPageSize = 20;
    private int _pageSize = 10;

    public string? MainCategory { get; set; }
    public string? SearchQuery { get; set; }

    public int PageNumber { get; set; } = 1;
    public int PageSize
    {
        get => _pageSize;
        set => _pageSize = (value > MaxPageSize) ? MaxPageSize : value;
    }

    public string OrderBy { get; set; } = "Name";

    // Data shaping
    public string? Fields { get; set; }
} ^uS5xL0EA

using System.Reflection;

public class PropertyCheckerService : IPropertyCheckerService
{
    public bool TypeHasProperties<T>(string? fields)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fields))
            return true;

        var fieldsAfterSplit = fields.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);

        foreach (var field in fieldsAfterSplit)
        {
            var propertyName = field.Trim();

            var propertyInfo = typeof(T).GetProperty(
                propertyName,
                BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance);

            if (propertyInfo is null)
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    }
} ^ny4JmZ6O

private string? CreateAuthorsResourceUri(
    AuthorsResourceParameters p,
    ResourceUriType type)
{
    return type switch
    {
        ResourceUriType.PreviousPage => Url.Link("GetAuthors", new
        {
            fields = p.Fields,
            orderBy = p.OrderBy,
            pageNumber = p.PageNumber - 1,
            pageSize = p.PageSize,
            mainCategory = p.MainCategory,
            searchQuery = p.SearchQuery
        }),
        ResourceUriType.NextPage => Url.Link("GetAuthors", new
        {
            fields = p.Fields,
            orderBy = p.OrderBy,
            pageNumber = p.PageNumber + 1,
            pageSize = p.PageSize,
            mainCategory = p.MainCategory,
            searchQuery = p.SearchQuery
        }),
        _ => Url.Link("GetAuthors", new
        {
            fields = p.Fields,
            orderBy = p.OrderBy,
            pageNumber = p.PageNumber,
            pageSize = p.PageSize,
            mainCategory = p.MainCategory,
            searchQuery = p.SearchQuery
        })
    };
} ^VCxNULuL

using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.Net.Http.Headers;

[HttpGet("{authorId}", Name = "GetAuthor")]
public async Task<IActionResult> GetAuthor(Guid authorId, string? fields,
    [FromHeader(Name = "Accept")] string? mediaType)
{
    // 1) Parse accept header
    if (!MediaTypeHeaderValue.TryParse(mediaType, out var parsedMediaType))
    {
        return BadRequest("Invalid Accept header.");
    }

    // 2) Fetch
    var authorFromRepo = await _courseLibraryRepository.GetAuthorAsync(authorId);
    if (authorFromRepo == null) return NotFound();

    var authorDto = _mapper.Map<AuthorDto>(authorFromRepo);

    // 3) Representation decision
    if (parsedMediaType.MediaType == "application/vnd.marvin.hateoas+json")
    {
        var links = CreateLinksForAuthor(authorId, fields);

        var shaped = authorDto.ShapeData(fields) as IDictionary<string, object?>;
        shaped!.Add("links", links);

        return Ok(shaped);
    }

    // default/plain representation
    return Ok(authorDto.ShapeData(fields));
} ^55rnmFfm
